A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE
Audit order: 161

Requirement ID: IMO-083
Source family: IMO
Verification mode: DIRECT_STATIC
Source document: International Code for Ships Operating in Polar Waters (Polar Code)
Source revision: Resolution MSC.385(94), adopted 21 Nov 2014; consolidated with MEPC.264(68), adopted 15 May 2015
Clause / section / page: 9.3.2.1.3 / Part I-A Chapter 9 - Navigational equipment / 27
Source URL: https://wwwcdn.imo.org/localresources/en/KnowledgeCentre/IndexofIMOResolutions/MSCResolutions/MSC.385%2894%29.pdf
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/MSC.385(94).pdf

Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-IMO-083-20260915T111601376970Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
For ships operating in areas and during periods where ice accretion is likely to occur, means to prevent the accumulation of ice on antennas required for navigation and communication shall be provided.
Normalized requirement:
IF ice_accretion_likely = true, THEN every required navigation or communication antenna shall have ice_accumulation_prevention_means_present = true.
Required inputs: Antenna inventory, ice-accretion applicability, and prevention arrangement

```turtle
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

ex:ship a nltl:ship ;
    nltl:hasRequiredNavigationOrCommunicationAntenna ex:antenna1,
        ex:antenna2 ;

    nltl:antennaIceAccumulationPreventionPresent true .
```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .

gen:IMO_083_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:or (
        gen:IMO_083_ApplicableBranch
        gen:IMO_083_NotApplicableBranch
    ) .

gen:IMO_083_ApplicableBranch
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceAccretionLikely ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "Ice-accretion applicability must be recorded as true for the applicable branch."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasRequiredNavigationOrCommunicationAntenna ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:IMO_083_AntennaShape ;
        sh:message "Each required navigation or communication antenna must provide ice-prevention evidence when ice accretion is likely."
    ] .

gen:IMO_083_NotApplicableBranch
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceAccretionLikely ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue false ;

    ] .

        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;

    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO_016_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasPolarShipCertificate ;
        sh:class nltl:polarShipCertificateForm ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:IMO_016_CertificateFormShape ;
        sh:message "The ship shall have a Polar Ship Certificate form conforming to the required model."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:certificateLanguage ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The certificate language is required to determine whether an approved translation is required."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:certificateLanguage ;
                sh:in ( "English" "French" "Spanish" )
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:approvedTranslationPresent ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:message "A certificate language other than English, French, or Spanish requires an approved translation."
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "If the certificate language is neither English, French, nor Spanish, an approved translation shall be present." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:certificateLanguage ?language .
                FILTER (STR(?language) NOT IN ("English", "French", "Spanish"))
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:approvedTranslationPresent ?translation .
                    FILTER (?translation = true)
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:IMO_016_CertificateFormShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:polarShipCertificateForm ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:certificateFormModel ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:appendixIPolarShipCertificateModel ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The Polar Ship Certificate shall use the Appendix I model."
    ] .

```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO_016_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasPolarShipCertificate ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class nltl:polarShipCertificateForm ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:certificateFormModel ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:appendixIPolarShipCertificateModel ;
                sh:message "The Polar Ship Certificate shall use the Appendix I model."
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:certificateLanguage ;
                sh:datatype xsd:string ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:in ( "English" "French" "Spanish" )
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:certificateLanguage ;
                sh:datatype xsd:string ;
                sh:minCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:approvedTranslationPresent ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:message "A certificate language other than English, French, or Spanish requires an approved translation."
            ]
        ]
    ) .

```

```turtle
gen:IMO_016_Shape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasPolarShipCertificate ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:class nltl:polarShipCertificateForm ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:certificateFormModel ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:appendixIPolarShipCertificateModel ;
                sh:message "The Polar Ship Certificate shall use the Appendix I model."
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:certificateLanguage ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:message "The certificate language must be recorded."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:certificateLanguage ;
                sh:in ( "English" "French" "Spanish" )
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:and (
                [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:approvedTranslationPresent ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                        sh:hasValue true ;
                        sh:message "A translation into English, French, or Spanish is required when another certificate language is used."
                    ]
                ]
                [
                    sh:sparql [
                        sh:select """
                            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                            SELECT ?this
                            WHERE {
                                ?this nltl:certificateLanguage ?language .
                                FILTER (?language NOT IN ("English", "French", "Spanish"))
                            }
                        """
                    ]
                ]
            )
        ]
    ) .
```

```turtle
    qudt:unit unit:M .
```

```turtle
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

                $this nltl:averageIcePressure ?pavgNode ;
                       nltl:span ?spanNode ;
                       nltl:loadPatchHeight ?loadHeightNode ;
                       nltl:peakPressureFactor ?ppfNode ;
                       nltl:shearArea ?shearAreaNode ;
                       nltl:yieldStrength ?yieldNode ;

                ?spanNode qudt:numericValue ?span .

                ?shearAreaNode qudt:numericValue ?shearArea .

                        nltl:requiredTransverseFrameShearArea ?requiredNode .

                ?spacingNode qudt:numericValue ?spacing .
                ?afNode qudt:numericValue ?af .
                ?requiredNode qudt:numericValue ?required .

                BIND (IF(?span < ?loadHeight, ?span, ?loadHeight) AS ?ll)
                BIND ((10000 * 0.5 * ?ll * ?spacing *
                       (?af * ?ppf * ?pavg) /
                       (0.577 * ?yield)) AS ?expectedRequired)
                BIND ((10000 * ?shearArea) AS ?actualShearCm2)

                FILTER (
                    ?actualShearCm2 < ?required ||
                    ABS(?required - ?expectedRequired) >
                    (0.000001 * ABS(?expectedRequired) + 0.000001)
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
                ?aNode qudt:numericValue ?a .
                ?bNode qudt:numericValue ?b .
                ?ppfNode qudt:numericValue ?ppf .
                ?awNode qudt:numericValue ?awM2 .
                ?syNode qudt:numericValue ?sy .

                ?member nltl:frameSpacing ?sNode ;
                        nltl:selectedHullAreaFactor ?afNode ;
                        nltl:requiredTransverseFrameShearArea ?atNode .

                ?sNode qudt:numericValue ?s .
                ?afNode qudt:numericValue ?af .
                ?atNode qudt:numericValue ?at .

                BIND(IF(?a <= ?b, ?a, ?b) AS ?ll)
                BIND((100 * 100 * 0.5 * ?ll * ?s * (?af * ?ppf * ?p) / (0.577 * ?sy)) AS ?expectedAt)
                BIND((ABS(?expectedAt) * 0.000001 + 0.000001) AS ?tolerance)
                BIND((?awM2 * 10000) AS ?awCm2)

                FILTER(
                    ABS(?at - ?expectedAt) > ?tolerance ||
                    ?awCm2 < ?at
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The required transverse frame shear area must equal the formula result within a relative tolerance of 1e-6, and the actual ship shear area must be at least that result." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                ?ship nltl:hasStructuralMember $this ;
                       nltl:averageIcePressure ?pNode ;
                       nltl:frameSpan ?aNode ;
                       nltl:loadPatchHeight ?bNode ;
                       nltl:peakPressureFactor ?ppfNode ;
                       nltl:shearArea ?awNode ;
                       nltl:yieldStrength ?syNode .

                $this nltl:frameSpacing ?sNode ;
                      nltl:selectedHullAreaFactor ?afNode ;
                      nltl:requiredTransverseFrameShearArea ?atNode .

                ?pNode qudt:numericValue ?p .
                ?aNode qudt:numericValue ?a .
                ?bNode qudt:numericValue ?b .
                ?ppfNode qudt:numericValue ?ppf .
                ?awNode qudt:numericValue ?aw .
                ?syNode qudt:numericValue ?sy .
                ?sNode qudt:numericValue ?s .
                ?afNode qudt:numericValue ?af .
                ?atNode qudt:numericValue ?at .

                BIND(IF(?a <= ?b, ?a, ?b) AS ?ll)
                BIND((100 * 100 * 0.5 * ?ll * ?s * ?af * ?ppf * ?p) / (0.577 * ?sy) AS ?expected)
                BIND(IF(ABS(?expected) * 0.000001 > 0.000000001,
                        ABS(?expected) * 0.000001,
                        0.000000001) AS ?tolerance)

                FILTER(
                    ABS(?at - ?expected) > ?tolerance ||
                    (?aw * 10000) < ?expected
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

Q5 Branches / boundaries: Are alternatives, exceptions, equality boundaries, and conditionals correct?
Q6 No extra restrictions: Does the shape avoid requirements not supported by the source?
Q7 Traceability / clarity: Is the implementation understandable and traceable to the cited evidence?
Record grades, reviewer identity, evidence, and notes in the workbook. Do not infer full-requirement correctness from these two cases.

REQUIREMENT 164: TRF-130

A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE

Clause / section / page: 7.2 / Sea inlet and cooling water systems / 62
Source URL: https://www.finlex.fi/en/authorities/regulations/traficom-transport-maritime/2021/47238
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/TRAFICOM.pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 8ab845347770c253433b8bad9b1fbe5d6ac71b3d07a1a28dae8a32cfde732ddc
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-130-20260915T141417279312Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
For this purpose, at least one cooling water inlet chest shall be arranged as follows: 1. The sea inlet shall be situated near the centreline of the ship and well aft, if possible. 2. Guidance for designing the volume of the chest shall be around one cubic metre for every 750 kW in engine output of the ship, including the output of auxiliary engines necessary for the operation of the ship. 3. The chest shall be sufficiently high to allow ice to accumulate above the inlet pipe. 4. A pipe for discharge cooling water, allowing full capacity discharge, shall be connected to the chest. 5. The open area of the strainer plates shall be no less than four (4) times the inlet pipe sectional area. If there are difficulties in meeting the requirements of paragraphs 2 and 3 above, two smaller chests may be arranged for alternating the intake and discharge of cooling water. Otherwise, the arrangements and situation shall be as above. Heating coils may be installed in the upper part of the sea chest.
Normalized requirement:
At least one cooling-water inlet chest shall satisfy: sea_inlet_location = near_centreline_and_well_aft_if_possible; recommended_chest_volume_m3 approximately total_relevant_engine_power_kW/750; ice_accumulation_space_above_inlet = true; full_capacity_discharge_pipe_connected = true; strainer_open_area_m2 >= 4*inlet_pipe_area_m2. If paragraphs 2 and 3 cannot be met, two smaller alternating intake/discharge chests may be used. Heating coils are optional.
Required inputs: mcrPower_kW, seaChestVolume_m3, strainerOpenAreaRatio

Test pattern: UNSPECIFIED

Full unmodified RDF Turtle:
```turtle
@prefix ex: <https://w3id.org/nltl/benchmark/traficom-batch-j/TRF-130-P01/> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

ex:ship a nltl:ship ;
    nltl:coolingWaterChestAlternativeArrangementUsed false ;
    nltl:coolingWaterChestVolumeAndHeightRequirementsCannotBeMet false ;
    nltl:hasComponent ex:chest1 .

ex:chest1 a nltl:inletChest ;
    nltl:fullCapacityDischargePipeConnected true ;
    nltl:iceAccumulationSpaceAboveInlet true ;
    nltl:inletPipeArea ex:chest1InletArea ;
    nltl:nearCentrelineAndWellAftIfPossible true ;
    nltl:recommendedChestVolume ex:chest1Recommended ;
    nltl:seaChestVolume ex:chest1Actual ;
    nltl:strainerOpenArea ex:chest1StrainerArea ;
    nltl:strainerOpenAreaRatio ex:chest1Ratio ;
    nltl:totalRelevantEnginePower ex:chest1Power .

ex:chest1Actual a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 2.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:M3 .

ex:chest1InletArea a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 1.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:M2 .

ex:chest1Power a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 1500.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:KiloW .

ex:chest1Ratio a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 4.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:UNITLESS .

ex:chest1Recommended a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 2.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:M3 .
```

```turtle
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;

            ] ;

                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [

        sh:minCount 1 ;

    sh:property [

        sh:minCount 1 ;

        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:minInclusive "4"^^xsd:decimal
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The inlet chest volume must be approximately total relevant engine power divided by 750, and the strainer open area must be at least four times the inlet pipe area." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:recommendedChestVolume ?recommendedQuantity .
                ?recommendedQuantity qudt:numericValue ?recommendedVolume .
                $this nltl:inletPipeArea ?pipeQuantity .
                ?pipeQuantity qudt:numericValue ?pipeArea .
                $this nltl:strainerOpenArea ?strainerQuantity .
                ?strainerQuantity qudt:numericValue ?strainerArea .
                ?ship nltl:hasComponent $this .
                ?ship nltl:totalRelevantEnginePower ?powerQuantity .
                ?powerQuantity qudt:numericValue ?totalPower .
                BIND(?totalPower / 750 AS ?expectedVolume)
                BIND(IF(ABS(?expectedVolume) * "0.000001"^^xsd:decimal > "0.000000001"^^xsd:decimal,
                        ABS(?expectedVolume) * "0.000001"^^xsd:decimal,
                        "0.000000001"^^xsd:decimal) AS ?volumeTolerance)
                FILTER(
                    ABS(?recommendedVolume - ?expectedVolume) > ?volumeTolerance
                    || ?strainerArea < 4 * ?pipeArea
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:seaChestVolume ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M3 ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:strainerOpenArea ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M2 ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:inletPipeArea ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M2 ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:strainerOpenAreaRatio ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The inlet chest volume and strainer open area ratio must satisfy the declared calculation." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
SELECT $this
WHERE {
  ?ship nltl:hasComponent $this ;
        nltl:totalRelevantEnginePower ?powerQ .
  $this nltl:recommendedChestVolume ?volumeQ ;
        nltl:strainerOpenArea ?openQ ;
        nltl:inletPipeArea ?pipeQ ;
        nltl:strainerOpenAreaRatio ?ratioQ .
  ?powerQ qudt:numericValue ?power .
  ?volumeQ qudt:numericValue ?volume .
  ?openQ qudt:numericValue ?open .
  ?pipeQ qudt:numericValue ?pipe .
  ?ratioQ qudt:numericValue ?ratio .
  FILTER(
    ABS(?volume - (?power / 750.0)) <=
      (ABS(?power / 750.0) * 0.000001 + 0.000000001)
  )
  FILTER(?open >= (4.0 * ?pipe))
  FILTER(
    ABS(?ratio - (?open / ?pipe)) <=
      (ABS(?open / ?pipe) * 0.000001 + 0.000000001)
  )
}
"""
    ] .

```

Original rationale: Multiple components protected.
Source clause: 6.6.1
Test pattern: UNSPECIFIED

Full unmodified RDF Turtle:
```turtle
@prefix ex: <https://w3id.org/nltl/benchmark/traficom-batch-h/TRF-104-P02/> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

ex:ship a nltl:ship ;
    nltl:hasPropellerShaftLineComponent ex:component0,
        ex:component1,
        ex:component2 ;
    nltl:pyramidStrengthPrinciple "blade loss shall not significantly damage other shaft-line components"^^xsd:string .

ex:component0 a nltl:propellerShaftLineComponent ;
    nltl:shaftLineComponentDamage false ;
    nltl:significantDamageFromBladeLoss false .

ex:component1 a nltl:propellerShaftLineComponent ;
    nltl:shaftLineComponentDamage false ;
    nltl:significantDamageFromBladeLoss false .

ex:component2 a nltl:propellerShaftLineComponent ;
    nltl:shaftLineComponentDamage false ;
    nltl:significantDamageFromBladeLoss false .


```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF104ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:pyramidStrengthPrinciple ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The propulsion line design shall identify the pyramid strength principle."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasPropellerShaftLineComponent ;
        sh:class nltl:propellerShaftLineComponent ;
        sh:node gen:TRF104ShaftLineComponentShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each linked propeller shaft line component shall be evaluated for damage from blade loss."
    ] .

gen:TRF104ShaftLineComponentShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:significantDamageFromBladeLoss ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:in ( false ) ;
        sh:message "Loss of the propeller blade shall not cause significant damage to the propeller shaft line component."
    ] .

```

Original source text:
Ships, with the exception of those solely operating in areas with 24 h daylight, shall be equipped with two remotely rotatable, narrow-beam search lights controllable from the bridge to provide lighting over an arc of 360 degrees, or other means to visually detect ice.
Normalized requirement:
IF solely_24_hour_daylight_operation = false, THEN (bridge_controlled_rotatable_narrow_beam_searchlight_count >= 2 AND searchlight_coverage_arc_deg >= 360) OR alternate_visual_ice_detection_means_approval_status = approved.

```turtle
                        sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
                        sh:minInclusive 2 ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:message "At least two bridge-controlled rotatable narrow-beam searchlights are required."
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:searchlightCoverageArc ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:node [
                            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                                sh:minInclusive "360"^^xsd:decimal ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1
                            ] ;
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                                sh:hasValue unit:DEG ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1
                            ]
                        ] ;
                        sh:message "Searchlights must provide coverage over an arc of at least 360 degrees."
                    ]
                ]
                [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:alternateVisualIceDetectionMeansApprovalStatus ;
                        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
                        sh:hasValue nltl:evidenceStateApproved ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:message "Approved alternative visual ice-detection means are required when the searchlight alternative is not demonstrated."
                    ]
                ]
            ) ;
            sh:message "A ship not operating solely in continuous daylight must provide either the required searchlights and coverage or approved alternative visual ice-detection means."
        ]
    ) .

```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:PowerQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloW> ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Power quantity must have exactly one unit of kilowatt."
    ] .

gen:ApplicableIceClassBranch
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:iceClassIb
            nltl:iceClassIc
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The applicable ice class must be IB or IC."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:applicableIceClassRegulationEdition ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:iceClassRuleEdition1985
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Ice class regulation edition 1985 must apply to the IB or IC case."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:maximumContinuousRatingPower ;
        sh:node gen:PowerQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Maximum continuous rating power is required for the applicable IB or IC case."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:requiredPowerUnder1985Rules ;
        sh:node gen:PowerQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Required power under the 1985 rules is required for the applicable IB or IC case."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Maximum continuous rating power must be at least the required power under the 1985 rules." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:maximumContinuousRatingPower ?maximumQuantity .
                ?maximumQuantity qudt:numericValue ?maximumValue ;
                                 qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloW> .
                $this nltl:requiredPowerUnder1985Rules ?requiredQuantity .
                ?requiredQuantity qudt:numericValue ?requiredValue ;
                                 qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloW> .
                FILTER (?maximumValue < ?requiredValue)
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:NonApplicableIceClassBranch
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
                sh:in (
                    nltl:iceClassIaSuper
                    nltl:iceClassIa
                    nltl:iceClassIi
                    nltl:iceClassIii
                ) ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:applicableIceClassRegulationEdition ;
                sh:in (
                    nltl:iceClassRegulationEdition2002
                    nltl:iceClassRegulationEdition2008
                    nltl:iceClassRegulationEdition2010
                    nltl:iceClassRegulationEdition2017
                    nltl:iceClassRegulationEdition2021
                ) ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ) .

gen:TRF023ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:iceClassIaSuper
            nltl:iceClassIa
            nltl:iceClassIb
            nltl:iceClassIc
            nltl:iceClassIi
            nltl:iceClassIii
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Exactly one canonical ice class value is required."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [ sh:node gen:ApplicableIceClassBranch ]
        [ sh:node gen:NonApplicableIceClassBranch ]
    ) .

```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-023> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "For a ship of ice class IB or IC subject to the 1985 ice class regulations, maximum continuous rating power must be at least the required power under the 1985 rules, with both quantities expressed in kW." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceClass ?iceClass ;
                      nltl:applicableIceClassRegulationEdition
                          nltl:iceClassRegulationEdition2002 ;
                      nltl:maximumContinuousRatingPower ?mcr ;
                      nltl:requiredPowerUnder1985Rules ?requiredPower .

                FILTER (
                    ?iceClass IN (
                        nltl:iceClassIb,
                        nltl:iceClassIc
                    )
                )

                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:maximumContinuousRatingPower ?validMcr .
                        ?validMcr qudt:numericValue ?mcrValue ;
                                  qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloW> .
                        FILTER (datatype(?mcrValue) = xsd:decimal)
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:requiredPowerUnder1985Rules ?validRequiredPower .
                        ?validRequiredPower qudt:numericValue ?requiredValue ;
                                             qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloW> .
                        FILTER (datatype(?requiredValue) = xsd:decimal)
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:maximumContinuousRatingPower ?validMcr ;
                              nltl:requiredPowerUnder1985Rules ?validRequiredPower .
                        ?validMcr qudt:numericValue ?mcrValue ;
                                 qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloW> .
                        ?validRequiredPower qudt:numericValue ?requiredValue ;
                                            qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloW> .
                        FILTER (
                            datatype(?mcrValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                            datatype(?requiredValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                            ?mcrValue >= ?requiredValue
                        )
                    }
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gs:I2_047_SteelRenewalIceStrengthenedShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:gaugedThickness ;
        sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:property [
            sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
            sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
            sh:minCount 1 ;
            sh:maxCount 1
        ] ;
        sh:property [
            sh:path qudt:unit ;
            sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> ;
            sh:minCount 1 ;
            sh:maxCount 1
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:netThickness ;
        sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:property [
            sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
            sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
            sh:minCount 1 ;
            sh:maxCount 1
        ] ;
        sh:property [
            sh:path qudt:unit ;
            sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> ;
            sh:minCount 1 ;
            sh:maxCount 1
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:steelRenewalRequired ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "steelRenewalRequired must be true exactly when gaugedThickness is less than netThickness plus 0.5 mm" ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:gaugedThickness ?gaugedQuantity ;
                      nltl:netThickness ?netQuantity ;
                      nltl:steelRenewalRequired ?renewal .

                ?gaugedQuantity qudt:numericValue ?gauged .
                ?netQuantity qudt:numericValue ?net .

                FILTER (
                    ?renewal != (?gauged < (?net + 0.0005))
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```
